<meta>This page continues the author's argument against the false expectations people project onto the Rosicrucian Brotherhood. Having previously dismissed the idea that the Order intends to force a political or religious conversion of the world, the author now addresses the specific texts often confused with the Brotherhood's actual platform.</meta>

->of the Fraternity R. C.  237<-

the Fraternity has never made such a declaration, nor can it be proven from their writings.

However, the **Reformation of the Whole World** <note>original: "Reformation der gantzen Welt"; This refers to the *Allgemeine und General Reformation*, a satirical text by the Italian author Trajano Boccalini that was confusingly published alongside the first Rosicrucian manifesto.</note> was attached to a treatise of the Fraternity, but it is in no way to be attributed to them. As I have understood from a credible and learned man, it was previously translated from the Italian and printed together with the **Fama** <note>The *Fama Fraternitatis* (1614), the foundational manifesto of the Rosicrucians.</note>, appearing as if it were something new and shared the same views as the Fraternity's own writings.

One does indeed read there of a **reformation of the arts** <note>In this period, "arts" refers to the *artes liberales*—the collective branches of science, philosophy, and academic knowledge.</note> which was attempted by their first founder <note>Christian Rosenkreuz, the Order’s legendary founder, who according to the manifestos lived in the 14th and 15th centuries.</note> almost two hundred and seventeen years ago—namely, around the year 1400. Even then, the **liberal arts** certainly required a reformation and improvement. This has indeed been put into practice by some in these, our latter times—specifically in **Germany**, to say nothing of other nations—by **Rudolph Agricola**, **Erasmus of Rotterdam** (as two exceptionally magnificent lights of all good arts), by **Doctor Luther**, **Philip Melanchthon**, and **Theophrastus**...

<meta>catchword: Para-</meta>

<summary>The author clarifies that the "General Reformation" text often bound with Rosicrucian manifestos is actually a separate Italian work not written by the Order. He acknowledges the need for a reform of knowledge but credits historical figures like Luther and Erasmus with doing that work.</summary>

The rich and the powerful rarely hear the truth told about them / or even if they do hear it / it is soon transformed into lies: For the reason / that **truth** and **equity** <term>Billichkeit</term> <note>A concept of justice based on fairness and the spirit of the law rather than just the strict letter of the law.</note> are plain / while wealth and power are dressed and surrounded with the clothes of **hypocrisy** and **flattery**. And here the **enlightenment of the understanding** matters more /

# Of the Laws

...than the conquering of the **human will**.

For just as this will cannot be conquered by men / so too can the **understanding** be enlightened by **God the Lord** alone: The Lord alone provides the **will** / and the **ability** / to whom we attribute this **Reformation**. Who would wish to convert the stubborn, hardened **Jews** to **Christianity**, even if he possessed the grace to perform miracles?

There are indeed several passages in **Holy Scripture** / which may be applied to such a conversion / as if they [the Jews] would receive from the **Gentiles** <note>original: *Heiden*</note> that for which they wait / but they are nevertheless somewhat obscure to human understanding / and secret **revelation** and **ecstasy** <note>original: *Verzuckung*</note> are of no use for investigating and interpreting such things / even if one believes he cannot be deceived in such matters. For experience and the possibility of such things speak against the idea that we might agree in words / and in the comparison / or **concordance** of the same / and the **figures** <term>Figuren</term> <note>In this context, "figures" refers to symbolic types, emblems, or prophetic allegories used to interpret the Bible.</note> aligned with them / in deed

...far from one another / and the great, famous **wisdom** is transformed into vain **folly**.

Regarding the **madmen** <note>original: *Wahnsinnigen*</note>, we do not wish to speak in this place; for they (who are also to be found among these people) are to be healed more with good **medicines** than with teachings and admonition, and are worthy of more **mercy** than mockery from their fellow men.
